# Storage operations runbook (consolidation period)

## Journal gap

The primary MKT01 journal (journal_mkt01_q3.log) lost the events with sequence numbers 35163 through 35594 inclusive.
For exactly that range the replica journal journal_mkt01_q3_b.log is authoritative. Outside the range the replica is a
copy and is not used.

## Entries that are not files

These records describe objects that are not files; report them as NOT_A_FILE:
- desktop.ini on ntfs volumes (ENG01, ENG02, LEG01);
- .DS_Store on MKT01 and AppleDouble names starting with ._ on LAB01;
- OBJ01 keys containing a _temporary/ segment, and OBJ01 listing rows with is_latest=false or delete_marker=true;
- entries below a lost+found directory on HR01 and OPS01;
- OPS01 rows whose size is N/A (directory placeholders);
- ARC01 catalog rows with flag X (spanned continuation records) and names ending .spanned;
- RND01 names ending .hsmstub (HSM stubs).

## Appendix M

Principal merges, partition B, follow.
